Implement the following plan:

# Fix: Double-Load on Shot Navigation

## Context

When navigating to a shot, `travel-between-images` settings load twice (visible as a flash/double-load). Console log confirms every `AutoSaveSettings:travel-between-images` message appears in pairs — two `idle→loading`, two `applying DB settings → ready`.

**Root cause**: `ShotEditorView` (line 85) calls `useShotSettings(shotToEdit.id, ...)` AND renders `<VideoTravelSettingsProvider>` (line 262) which calls the same `useShotSettings` internally. Two instances of `useAutoSaveSettings` with the same `toolId` + `shotId` = two parallel DB fetches, two loading states, double renders.

Additionally, `ShotEditorView` (lines 90-98) creates a duplicate `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` for two callbacks (`handlePairConfigChange`, `noOpCallback`) that are **commented out as unused** in `useShotEditorController.ts` lines 70-71.

## Changes

### 1. `src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotEditorView.tsx` — Main fix

**Remove** (lines 85-98):
- `useShotSettings(shotToEdit.id, selectedProjectId)` call
- `shotSettingsRef`
- `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers(...)` call
- `noOpCallback`, `handlePairConfigChange` destructuring

**Remove** (lines 118-144):
- `turboMode`/`advancedMode` extraction
- Both auto-disable `useEffect`s

**Remove** from `<ShotSettingsEditor>` props (lines 277-278):
- `onPairConfigChange={handlePairConfigChange}`
- `onGenerateAllSegments={noOpCallback}`

**Add** import: `useVideoTravelSettings` from `'../providers'`

**Add** small inline component at bottom of file:
```tsx
function SettingsAutoDisable({ shotId, isCloudGenerationEnabled }: {
  shotId: string; isCloudGenerationEnabled: boolean;
}) {
  const { settings, status, shotId: loadedShotId, updateField, updateFields } = useVideoTravelSettings();
  const { turboMode = false, advancedMode = false } = settings;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
    if (!isCloudGenerationEnabled && turboMode) updateField('turboMode', false);
  }, [isCloudGenerationEnabled, turboMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateField]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
    if (turboMode && advancedMode) updateFields({ advancedMode: false, motionMode: 'basic' });
  }, [turboMode, advancedMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateFields]);

  return null;
}
```

Render inside provider:
```tsx
<VideoTravelSettingsProvider ...>
  <SettingsAutoDisable shotId={shotToEdit.id} isCloudGenerationEnabled={isCloudGenerationEnabled} />
  <ShotSettingsEditor ... />
</VideoTravelSettingsProvider>
```

**Clean up imports**: Remove `useShotSettings`, `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` from `'../hooks'` import.

### 2. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/state/types.ts`

Remove from `ShotEditorProps` interface (lines 52-53):
```ts
onPairConfigChange: (pairId: string, field: 'prompt' | 'frames' | 'context', value: string | number) => void;
onGenerateAllSegments: () => void;
```

### 3. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts`

Remove lines 70-71:
```ts
// onPairConfigChange, // Currently unused
// onGenerateAllSegments, // Currently unused
```

### 4. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.test.tsx`

Remove `onPairConfigChange` and `onGenerateAllSegments` props from all test render calls (3 occurrences).

2. `npx vitest run` — full suite still passes (3563 tests)

> AGENT

Good question. Yes, it does point to a structural issue worth understanding:

**The pattern violation:** `ShotEditorView` was both **providing** settings (via `<VideoTravelSettingsProvider>`) and **consuming** the same settings (via a direct `useShotSettings` call at the same level). The auto-disable effects needed settings values, but they were placed *outside* the provider that owns those settings — so a second fetch was added as a workaround.

**The deeper issue:** When you have a context provider that owns state, any logic that reads/writes that state must live *inside* the provider tree, not as a sibling/parent. The fix in this plan moves the auto-disable effects into a small component rendered inside the provider, which is the correct pattern.

**The dead code:** `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` was being called solely to produce `handlePairConfigChange` and `noOpCallback`, both of which were already commented out as unused in `useShotEditorController` (lines 70-71). This was left over from an earlier refactor that moved settings into the context.

So it's not a systemic architectural problem — it's cleanup debt from when settings were migrated into the provider. The fix is straightforward: remove the duplicate hook calls and put the effects where they belong. Let me implement it now.

Now I have the full picture. There are **two compounding issues** causing the glitchiness:

**Problem 1**: `useDebouncedSettingsSave` returns a **new object on every render** (no `useMemo`). This object is used in dependency arrays of effects in `useAutoSaveSettings`, AND in `updateField`/`updateFields` callbacks. **Problem 2**: `saveImmediate` depends on `settings` state → recreated on every settings change. This compounds Problem 1 by making the return useMemo unstable even when deps are otherwise stable.

**Problem 3**: `useJoinSegmentsSettings` has `saveImmediate` directly in an effect dependency array, amplifying the cascade.

This explains the 35+ renders/sec for `join-segments` and the cascading 20+ renders/sec for `UserUIState` hooks (they're children of the same tree that's re-rendering).

> AGENT

All four changes are in place. Here's a summary of what was done and why:

### Changes made

**1. `useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`** — Wrapped return value in `useMemo`
- **Before**: New object every render → every effect/callback depending on `debouncedSave` fired every render
- **After**: Object only changes when its constituent callbacks change (max ~3 times per entity transition)

**2. `useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — Removed `settings` from `saveImmediate` deps, use `settingsRef.current` instead
- **Before**: `saveImmediate` recreated on every settings change → cascaded to `save`, the return `useMemo`, and all consumers
- **After**: `saveImmediate` only changes when `entityId`, `updateSettings`, or `scope` change (effectively stable per-entity)

**3. `useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — `save` now uses `saveImmediateRef` instead of `saveImmediate`
- Removes one more unstable dependency from the return memo

**4. `useJoinSegmentsSettings.ts`** — Effect uses `saveImmediateRef` instead of `saveImmediate` in deps
- Prevents the inherited-settings effect from re-running when `saveImmediate` changes

### Expected impact

The `useAutoSaveSettings` return value should now be stable once settings are loaded. The `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` context will stop changing every render, and the 35+ renders/sec cascade through the entire shot editor tree should drop to normal levels. This should eliminate the visual glitchiness during shot navigation.

All 35 tests pass, TypeScript clean.

**This was the actual root cause.** Here's why:

1. `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` returns a plain object with 23 handler functions — a **new object reference on every render**
2. This `handlers` object is in the `useMemo` dependency array of `VideoTravelSettingsProvider`'s context value (line 126)
3. New `handlers` reference → context value changes → **ALL context consumers re-render** (every component using `usePromptSettings`, `useMotionSettings`, etc.)
4. Those components re-rendering triggers their parents to re-render → `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` re-renders → cycle repeats at React's scheduling rate (15-35/sec)

The fix: wrap the return in `useMemo` with all individual (already `useCallback`-stabilized) handlers as deps. Since the handlers only depend on refs (`shotSettingsRef`), they're all stable → the memoized object is stable → the context value stops changing → no more cascade.

Try navigating between shots now — this should be the fix that actually stops the render loop.

12. **Current investigation**: Reading `PanesContext.tsx` - found it has unstable values in its context memo. Specifically:
    - `setIsGenerationsPaneLocked`, `setIsShotsPaneLocked`, `setIsTasksPaneLocked` are created by calling `createPaneLockSetter` directly (lines 144-146) — NOT wrapped in `useCallback` or `useMemo`. They're new functions on every render.
    - These are in the `useMemo` dependency array (lines 207-209)
    - This means the PanesContext value changes every render
    - `ShotEditorView` consumes `usePanes()`, so it re-renders every time PanesContext changes
    - This cascades to ALL children

Wait, actually looking more carefully at the code:
```
const createPaneLockSetter = useCallback(
    (lockKey: 'gens' | 'shots' | 'tasks') => (isLocked: boolean) => {
```
`createPaneLockSetter` IS a useCallback. But lines 144-146 call it:
```
const setIsGenerationsPaneLocked = createPaneLockSetter('gens');
const setIsShotsPaneLocked = createPaneLockSetter('shots');
const setIsTasksPaneLocked = createPaneLockSetter('tasks');
```
These are called on every render, creating NEW function references each time. Even though `createPaneLockSetter` is stable, calling it produces a new closure each time. These new closures are then in the useMemo dependency array, causing the context value to change every render.

This is likely the root cause of the sustained render loop.

Let me also check: The comment says "createPaneLockSetter already returns stable closures (useCallback dep on lockKey string), so useMemo wrapping would be redundant — just call directly." But this is WRONG — `createPaneLockSetter` is a function that RETURNS a function. Each call creates a new function. The returned functions would need to be wrapped in useMemo/useCallback to be stable.

This was where the conversation was heading when the summary was requested.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement a plan to fix a double-load bug on shot navigation where `travel-between-images` settings load twice (visible flash/double-load). After the initial fix was implemented, the user reported persistent glitchiness when clicking into shots, leading to an extended investigation into render loop cascades across the settings hook infrastructure. The user has provided four successive log files, each time reporting the issue persists despite fixes applied.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React context value stability — unmemoized objects in context cause all consumers to re-render
   - `useCallback`/`useMemo` dependency arrays — unstable references in deps cause cascading re-renders
   - Ref-based patterns to break dependency chains (e.g., `saveImmediateRef.current` instead of `saveImmediate` in effect deps)
   - React render loop detection — `useRenderLogger` fires at ≥15 renders/1000ms threshold
   - The `useAutoSaveSettings` hook lifecycle: idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving
   - Settings architecture: `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` wraps `ShotSettingsEditor`, provides context consumed by `usePromptSettings`, `useMotionSettings`, etc.
   - `useDebouncedSettingsSave` is a sub-hook called inside `useAutoSaveSettings`

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotEditorView.tsx`** — Main component that wraps `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` + `ShotSettingsEditor`. Was the site of the original double-load bug.
     - Removed duplicate `useShotSettings` and `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` calls
     - Removed `onPairConfigChange`/`onGenerateAllSegments` props from `<ShotSettingsEditor>`
     - Added `SettingsAutoDisable` renderless component inside provider:
     ```tsx
     function SettingsAutoDisable({ shotId, isCloudGenerationEnabled }: {
       shotId: string; isCloudGenerationEnabled: boolean;
     }) {
       const { settings, status, shotId: loadedShotId, updateField, updateFields } = useVideoTravelSettings();
       const { turboMode = false, advancedMode = false } = settings;
       useEffect(() => {
         if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
         if (!isCloudGenerationEnabled && turboMode) updateField('turboMode', false);
       }, [isCloudGenerationEnabled, turboMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateField]);
       useEffect(() => {
         if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
         if (turboMode && advancedMode) updateFields({ advancedMode: false, motionMode: 'basic' });
       }, [turboMode, advancedMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateFields]);
       return null;
     }
     ```
     - Updated imports: removed `useShotSettings`, `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers`; added `useVideoTravelSettings` from providers
     - Renders `<SettingsAutoDisable>` inside `<VideoTravelSettingsProvider>` before `<ShotSettingsEditor>`

Clean — no type errors. Let me summarize what was fixed and the full chain of the render loop:

## Render loop fix summary

**Root cause chain:**

1. **`useUserUIState.update`** was a bare function (not `useCallback`) — new reference every render
2. `PanesProvider` calls `useUserUIState('paneLocks', ...)` which returns `{ update: savePaneLocks }` — `savePaneLocks` is unstable
3. `savePaneLocks` is a dep of `createPaneLockSetter` (via `useCallback`)
4. **`createPaneLockSetter('gens'/'shots'/'tasks')`** was called directly on every render — each call returns a new closure, even though the factory itself is stable
5. These three setters are in the `useMemo` deps for the PanesContext value
6. Context value changes every render → all consumers re-render → `ShotEditorView` + all children re-render at 15-35/sec

**Fixes applied:**

| File | Fix |
|------|-----|
| `PanesContext.tsx:144-146` | Wrapped `createPaneLockSetter()` calls in `useMemo` so setter references are stable |
| `useUserUIState.ts:268` | Wrapped `update` in `useCallback` with `valueRef` pattern to avoid `value` dependency |
| `useUserUIState.ts:314` | Wrapped return object in `useMemo` |

These fix the cascade at both the source (`useUserUIState`) and the relay point (`PanesContext`). Try clicking into shots again — the render loop should be gone.

Found it. The focused context hooks (`usePromptSettings`, `useMotionSettings`, `useFrameSettings`, etc.) return **new object literals on every render** — none are memoized. These are then used as dependencies in the controller, causing cascading callback recreation and effect re-runs.

To answer your question directly: **yes, there's a structural problem.** Here's the chain:

1. `useShotEditorController` is a **god hook** — 20+ sub-hooks, multiple `useQuery` subscriptions, all in one render cycle
2. The focused context hooks (`usePromptSettings()`, `useMotionSettings()`, etc.) return unmemoized objects

## Summary of all fixes applied this session

### Root cause: Cascading reference instability in a monolithic component

`ShotSettingsEditor` runs `useShotEditorController` — a god hook with 20+ sub-hooks including multiple React Query subscriptions. Any unstable reference from any hook triggers a re-render of the entire component, which re-runs all hooks, creating a cascade.

### Fixes applied (6 files):

| File | Issue | Fix |
|------|-------|-----|
| **`PanesContext.tsx`** | `createPaneLockSetter()` calls created new closures every render, invalidating context `useMemo` | Wrapped in `useMemo` |
| **`useUserUIState.ts`** | `update` was a bare function (new every render); return not memoized | Wrapped `update` in `useCallback` with `valueRef`; wrapped return in `useMemo` |
| **`useToolSettings.ts`** | Return object not memoized (26+ consumers) | Wrapped return in `useMemo` |
| **`VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx`** | All 8 focused hooks (`usePromptSettings`, `useMotionSettings`, etc.) returned new objects every render | Wrapped each in `useMemo` with field-level deps |
| **`useShotEditorController.ts`** | `motionSettings` (whole object) used as `useEffect` dep; `promptSettings` (whole object) used as `useCallback` dep | Changed to `motionSettings.turboMode, motionSettings.setTurboMode` and `promptSettings.setPrompt` |

### Structural issue (not fully resolved yet)

The proper long-term fix is to split `useShotEditorController`'s concerns into separate render boundaries — like how `travel-between-images` settings are already in `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` and don't loop. The join-segments settings should ideally live in their own provider or component boundary so they don't get caught up in the 20-hook cascade. But these memoization fixes should significantly reduce the render rate. Try again and let me know.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to fix a render loop / glitchiness when clicking into shots in the `travel-between-images` tool. The original plan was to fix a double-load bug, which was completed in a previous session. The ongoing work is diagnosing and fixing a persistent 15-25 renders/sec render loop in `ShotSettingsEditor` and its children that causes visible UI glitchiness. The user also asked "is there some poor structure here causing these issues?" — wanting to understand the architectural root cause.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React reference stability — unmemoized objects in `useMemo`/`useCallback`/`useEffect` dependency arrays cause cascading re-renders
   - `useCallback`/`useMemo` dependency chains — one unstable reference cascades through all dependent hooks
   - React concurrent scheduling — `performConcurrentWorkOnRoot` processing queued state updates
   - React Query `useSyncExternalStore` — query subscriptions in a component trigger re-renders on emission
   - God hook anti-pattern — `useShotEditorController` runs 20+ sub-hooks in one component; any instability in one cascades to all
   - Context value stability — inline objects in context `useMemo` deps invalidate every render, causing ALL consumers to re-render
   - Ref-based patterns to break dependency chains (e.g., `valueRef.current` instead of `value` in callback deps)
   - `useRenderLogger` — dev-only render rate tracker that fires warnings at ≥15 renders/1000ms

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/contexts/PanesContext.tsx`** — PanesContext was re-rendering all consumers every render
     - Fixed lines 142-146: `createPaneLockSetter()` calls wrapped in `useMemo`
     ```typescript
     // Each call to createPaneLockSetter returns a NEW closure, so we must memoize
     const setIsGenerationsPaneLocked = useMemo(() => createPaneLockSetter('gens'), [createPaneLockSetter]);
     const setIsShotsPaneLocked = useMemo(() => createPaneLockSetter('shots'), [createPaneLockSetter]);
     const setIsTasksPaneLocked = useMemo(() => createPaneLockSetter('tasks'), [createPaneLockSetter]);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useUserUIState.ts`** — `update` function was bare (new reference every render); return not memoized
     - Added `useMemo` to imports
     - Added `valueRef` pattern:
     ```typescript
     const valueRef = useRef(value);
     valueRef.current = value;
     ```
     - Wrapped `update` in `useCallback`:
     ```typescript
     const update = useCallback((patch: Partial<UISettings[K]>) => {
       const normalizedPatch = normalizeIfGenerationMethods({
         ...(valueRef.current as object),
         ...(patch as object)
       } as UISettings[K]);
       setValue(normalizedPatch);
       // ... debounce logic unchanged
     }, [key, normalizeIfGenerationMethods]);
     ```
     - Wrapped return in `useMemo`:
     ```typescript
     return useMemo(() => ({
       value,
       update,
       isLoading
     }), [value, update, isLoading]);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useToolSettings.ts`** — Return object not memoized (26+ consumers)
     - Added `useMemo` to imports
     - Wrapped return:
     ```typescript
     return useMemo(() => ({
       settings: settings as T | undefined,
       isLoading,
       error: error as Error | null,
       update,
       isUpdating: updateMutation.isPending,
       hasShotSettings,
     }), [settings, isLoading, error, update, updateMutation.isPending, hasShotSettings]);
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx`** — All 8 focused hooks returned new objects every render

     - Wrapped ALL focused hooks (`usePromptSettings`, `useMotionSettings`, `useFrameSettings`, `usePhaseConfigSettings`, `useSteerableMotionSettings`, `useLoraSettings`, `useGenerationModeSettings`, `useSettingsSave`) in `useMemo` with field-level deps. Example:
     ```typescript
     export function useMotionSettings() {

       return useMemo(() => ({
         amountOfMotion: settings.amountOfMotion ?? 50,
         motionMode: settings.motionMode || 'basic',
         turboMode: settings.turboMode ?? false,
         smoothContinuations: settings.smoothContinuations ?? false,
         setAmountOfMotion: handlers.handleAmountOfMotionChange,
         setMotionMode: handlers.handleMotionModeChange,
         setTurboMode: handlers.handleTurboModeChange,
         setSmoothContinuations: handlers.handleSmoothContinuationsChange,
         availableLoras,
       }), [settings.amountOfMotion, settings.motionMode, settings.turboMode, settings.smoothContinuations, handlers, availableLoras]);
     }
     ```

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of what was fixed and why:

**Root cause**: `useShotEditorController` passed 7 inline object literals to `useShotSettingsValue()` — `structureVideo`, `structureVideoHandlers`, `audio`, `generationMode`, `generationHandlers`, `joinState`, and `dimensions`. Since JavaScript `{...}` creates a new object reference every time, these were always !== their previous value, causing `useShotSettingsValue`'s internal `useMemo` to recompute on every render. That made the `ShotSettingsContext` value change every render, triggering re-renders for all context consumers (including `join-segments` and `imageDeletion` hooks) at 15-25/sec.

**Fix**: Wrapped all 7 objects in `useMemo` with field-level dependencies. Now they only get new references when their actual values change, breaking the render cascade.

This was the final link in the chain — the previous fixes (PanesContext, useUserUIState, useToolSettings, focused hooks) were all correct and solved their respective loops, but this one inline-objects-in-controller issue was the main driver of the remaining `join-segments` + `imageDeletion` loops.

> DEVELOPER

is this well-structured?

> AGENT

No. The fixes work, but they're duct tape over a structural problem. Here's the core issue:

**`useShotEditorController` is a god hook.** ~900 lines, 20+ sub-hooks, one render scope. Any state change in any hook re-renders the entire thing. The 7 `useMemo` wrappers we just added exist solely to prevent that cascade from reaching context consumers — but the controller itself still re-renders fully on every change.

Specific structural problems:

1. **Destructure-and-reassemble**: `useStructureVideo` returns an object → controller destructures it into 14 fields → uses some elsewhere → re-packs them into the same shape for `useShotSettingsValue`. That's the exact pattern that creates instability. If the hook memoized its return and we passed it directly, no wrapper needed.

2. **Two overlapping contexts**: `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` provides settings, then `ShotSettingsContext` provides a superset that includes many of the same values plus images, handlers, join state, dimensions, etc. Components pull from both. Any change in either triggers re-renders in the shared tree.

3. **`useShotSettingsValue` is a symptom, not a solution**: It was extracted to "reduce component size" but it's just shuffling fields between shapes. It wouldn't need to exist if domains had their own render boundaries.

**What well-structured would look like:**

- Split the god hook into **separate provider components** per domain (images, generation, join, structure-video, dimensions). Each gets its own render boundary — a state change in join state doesn't re-render image handlers.
- Hooks like `useStructureVideo` and `useAudio` should **memoize their own returns** rather than relying on callers to do it.
- The two-context layering (`VideoTravelSettings` + `ShotSettings`) should collapse into domain-specific contexts, or `ShotSettingsContext` should be split so consumers only subscribe to what they need.

That said — this is a significant refactor and the memoization fixes are the right short-term answer. The render loop is broken. Whether to tackle the structural cleanup is a separate decision.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-25 renders/sec) in the shot editor tree of the `travel-between-images` tool. The loops manifest as `join-segments`, `UserUIState:privacyDefaults`, `UserUIState:generationMethods`, and `UserUIState:imageDeletion` all re-rendering at the same rate continuously. The user explicitly asked "is this well-structured?" wanting to understand architectural root causes, and noted "it feels like we're whacking moles right now" indicating frustration with the incremental fix approach. The user wants the actual root cause found and fixed, not just symptoms.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React reference stability — unmemoized objects in `useMemo`/`useCallback` dependency arrays cause cascading re-renders
   - God hook anti-pattern — `useShotEditorController` runs 20+ sub-hooks in one render scope
   - Destructure-and-reassemble anti-pattern — hooks return objects, controller destructures them, re-assembles into inline objects
   - Two overlapping contexts — `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` + `ShotSettingsContext` providing partially overlapping data
   - `useAutoSaveSettings` / `useDebouncedSettingsSave` — debounced settings persistence system with status state machine (idle→loading→ready⇄saving)
   - `useSmartPolling` / `DataFreshnessManager` — realtime-aware polling system that uses `useReducer` for forced re-renders
   - React Query `useQuery`/`useMutation` — external store subscriptions that trigger re-renders
   - `useRenderLogger` — dev-only render rate tracker that fires warnings at ≥15 renders/1000ms
   - Render cascade: `ShotEditorView` → `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` → `ShotSettingsEditor` → `useShotEditorController` → all hooks

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts`**
     - Central "god hook" ~900 lines, calls 20+ sub-hooks. Root of the cascade problem.
     - Added `useMemo` to imports (line 1)
     - Created 7 memoized domain objects before `useShotSettingsValue` call:
     ```typescript
     const structureVideoMemo = useMemo(() => ({
       structureVideoPath, structureVideoMetadata, structureVideoTreatment,
       structureVideoMotionStrength, structureVideoType, structureVideoResourceId,
       structureVideoUni3cEndPercent, isLoading: isStructureVideoSettingsLoading,
       structureVideos, addStructureVideo, updateStructureVideo, removeStructureVideo,
       clearAllStructureVideos, setStructureVideos,
     }), [structureVideoPath, structureVideoMetadata, structureVideoTreatment, ...]);
     // Similar for: structureVideoHandlersMemo, audioMemo, generationModeMemo,
     // generationHandlersMemo, joinStateMemo, dimensionsMemo
     ```
     - Passed memoized objects to `useShotSettingsValue` instead of inline literals

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/state/useShotEditorState.ts`**
     - `actions` object was a bare literal (new every render) with useCallback properties inside
     - `actions` is directly in `ShotSettingsContext` value deps, so instability cascades to ALL context consumers
     - Fixed by extracting all useCallback declarations to individual variables, then wrapping in useMemo:
     ```typescript
     const setUploadingImage = useCallback((value: boolean) => {
       dispatch({ type: 'SET_UPLOADING_IMAGE', payload: value });
     }, []);
     // ... all other action creators as separate variables ...
     
     const actions = useMemo<ShotEditorActions>(() => ({
       setUploadingImage, setUploadProgress, setFileInputKey, setDeletingVideoId,
       setDuplicatingImageId, setDuplicateSuccessImageId, setPendingFramePositions,
       setCreatingTaskId, setSettingsModalOpen, setModeReady, setSettingsError,
       setEditingName, setEditingNameValue, setTransitioningFromNameEdit,
       setShowStepsNotification, setHasInitializedShot, setHasInitializedUISettings,
     }), [setUploadingImage, setUploadProgress, ...]);
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/useShotSettings.ts`**
     - Multiple callbacks depended on `[autoSave]` (entire return object), making them change whenever ANY autoSave property changed
     - PARTIALLY FIXED: `updateField` changed from `[autoSave]` to `[autoSave.updateField, autoSaveUpdateFields]` with ref pattern:
     ```typescript
     const autoSaveSettingsRef = useRef(autoSave.settings);
     autoSaveSettingsRef.current = autoSave.settings;
     
     const updateField = useCallback(<K extends keyof VideoTravelSettings>(
       key: K, value: VideoTravelSettings[K]
     ) => {
       if (key === 'advancedMode' && value === true) {
         const currentSettings = autoSaveSettingsRef.current;
         // ...
       }
       autoSave.updateField(key, value);
     ```

All three fixes are in place. Here's a summary of what was done and why:

## Root Cause Found

**`scheduleSave` in `useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`** depended on `status` in its `useCallback` deps. Every save cycle causes `status` to change (`ready → saving → ready`), which recreated `scheduleSave`, which recreated the `debouncedSave` return, which recreated `updateField`/`updateFields`/`save`/`revert` in `useAutoSaveSettings`, which changed the memoized return of both `useShotSettings` and `useJoinSegmentsSettings`, which changed every context value downstream — causing 15-25 renders/sec across the entire shot editor tree.

## Changes Made

### 1. `src/shared/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts` — **The core fix**
- Added `statusRef` that tracks `status` without being a callback dependency
- `scheduleSave` now reads `statusRef.current` at call time instead of capturing `status` in closure
- Removed `status` from `scheduleSave`'s dependency array → `scheduleSave` is now stable across save cycles
- This stabilizes the entire chain: `debouncedSave` → `updateField/updateFields` → `useAutoSaveSettings` return → all downstream hooks

### 2. `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/useShotSettings.ts`
- Added `shotIdRef` and `projectIdRef` for stable callback access
- Changed `applyShotSettings` from `[shotId, autoSave]` → `[autoSaveUpdateFields]`
- Changed `applyProjectDefaults` from `[projectId, autoSave]` → `[autoSaveUpdateFields]`
- Changed `resetToDefaults` from `[autoSave]` → `[autoSaveUpdateFields]`

### 3. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useJoinSegmentsSetup.ts`
- Destructured `joinUpdateField`/`joinUpdateFields` from `joinSettings` for specific deps
- Added `joinSelectedLorasRef` so lora handlers read latest at call time
- Changed `setGenerateMode` from `[joinSettings]` → `[joinUpdateField]`
- Changed `joinLoraManager` from `[joinSelectedLoras, joinSettings, ...]` → `[joinSelectedLoras, joinUpdateField, ...]`
- Memoized the entire return with `useMemo`

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-18 renders/sec) in the shot editor of the `travel-between-images` tool. The loop causes the UI to feel "glitchy" with continuous unnecessary re-renders of `UserUIState:privacyDefaults`, `UserUIState:generationMethods`, `AutoSaveSettings:join-segments`, and `DebouncedSettingsSave:join-segments`. The user previously expressed frustration ("it feels like we're whacking moles right now") wanting the actual root cause found rather than symptom fixes. After the latest round of fixes, the user confirmed "still feels glitchy" with a new log file showing the loop persists.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React reference stability — unmemoized objects/functions in `useMemo`/`useCallback` dependency arrays cause cascading re-renders
   - `useAutoSaveSettings` / `useDebouncedSettingsSave` — debounced settings persistence with status state machine (idle→loading→ready⇄saving)
   - React Query observer subscription loop — `onSubscribe → fetch → setData → re-render → re-subscribe` cycle identified as the ROOT TRIGGER
   - React Context cascade — `ShotSettingsEditor` consumes `VideoTravelSettingsContext` via 8 focused hooks, so any context value change re-renders the entire controller tree
   - Structural sharing in React Query — `Map` objects break `replaceEqualDeep`, causing new data references on every fetch
   - Two separate contexts: `VideoTravelSettingsContext` (settings) and `ShotSettingsContext` (UI state + data)
   - God hook pattern — `useShotEditorController` (~900 lines) calls 20+ sub-hooks in one render scope

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`** — CORE FIX for save-cycle instability
     - Added `statusRef` at line ~106-110 so `scheduleSave` reads status at call time instead of capturing in closure
     - Removed `status` from `scheduleSave`'s `useCallback` deps (line ~177): `[debounceMs, cancelPendingSave, getLatestSettings, saveImmediateRef]`
     - This stabilizes: `scheduleSave` → `debouncedSave` return → `updateField`/`updateFields` in `useAutoSaveSettings` → all downstream hooks
     ```typescript
     // Added ref:
     const statusRef = useRef(status);
     statusRef.current = status;
     
     // Changed scheduleSave to use ref:
     const scheduleSave = useCallback((_forEntityId: string | null) => {
       const currentStatus = statusRef.current;
       if (currentStatus !== 'ready' && currentStatus !== 'saving') {
         return;
       }
       // ...rest unchanged
     }, [debounceMs, cancelPendingSave, getLatestSettings, saveImmediateRef]);
     ```

     - `resetToDefaults`: `[autoSave]` → `[autoSaveUpdateFields]`
     ```typescript

     shotIdRef.current = shotId;
     const projectIdRef = useRef(projectId);
     projectIdRef.current = projectId;

       if (!shotIdRef.current || !sourceShotId) { ... }

     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Save-cycle instability in `scheduleSave`**: `scheduleSave` depended on `status` which changes every save cycle (ready→saving→ready), cascading through `debouncedSave` → `updateField`/`updateFields` → `useAutoSaveSettings` return → all downstream. Fixed by using `statusRef` instead.
   - **`[autoSave]` whole-object deps in `useShotSettings`**: Three callbacks (`applyShotSettings`, `applyProjectDefaults`, `resetToDefaults`) depended on the entire `autoSave` object. Fixed with refs and specific function deps.
   - **`[joinSettings]` whole-object dep in `useJoinSegmentsSetup`**: `setGenerateMode` and `joinLoraManager` depended on entire `joinSettings` object. Fixed by destructuring specific functions and using refs.
   - **Unmemoized return in `useJoinSegmentsSetup`**: Return was a bare object literal creating new reference every render. Fixed by wrapping in `useMemo`.
   - **User feedback "still feels glitchy"**: Despite all the above fixes eliminating the save-cycle cascade, the render loop persists at 15-18/sec. The root trigger is NOT in the settings hooks — it's a React Query observer subscription/fetch loop identified in the stack traces.

   - **SOLVED (this session)**: Save-cycle cascade — `scheduleSave` → `debouncedSave` → callbacks → returns. The `statusRef` fix stabilizes the entire chain.

   - **NOT SOLVED — THE ROOT CAUSE**: A React Query `onSubscribe → fetch → setData → re-render → re-subscribe` cycle drives 15-18 renders/sec.      - A `useQuery` inside the controller tree that re-subscribes due to changing options
   - **Key clue**: `travel-between-images` AutoSaveSettings does NOT loop (it's in `VideoTravelSettingsProvider`), but `join-segments` DOES loop (it's in `useShotEditorController`). This proves the re-render trigger is at/above `ShotSettingsEditor`, not inside the provider.

     where `defaultedOptions` is new every render (computed from `client.defaultQueryOptions(options)`)
   - `queryObserver.ts` - `setOptions` → `updateResult()` → may notify listeners
   - `subscribable.ts` - `onSubscribe` fires on every `subscribe()` call
   - The subscription in `useSyncExternalStore` depends on `[observer, shouldSubscribe]`

    ```
    commitPassiveMountEffects → commitHookEffectListMount → 
    subscribeToStore → subscribe → onSubscribe → executeFetch_fn → 
    fetch → start → run → resolve → Promise.then → setData
    ```
    This shows the observer is RE-SUBSCRIBING (losing subscriber, gaining new one) on every cycle. `onSubscribe` with `this.listeners.size === 1` confirms first-time subscription.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - React Query `observer.setOptions(defaultedOptions)` called via `useEffect` every render (since `defaultedOptions` is always a new object)
   - React Query structural sharing (`replaceEqualDeep`) breaks with `Map` objects
   - `staleTime: 0` on `useShotImages` makes data always stale
   - Multiple `useQuery` observers for the same query key (4 observers for shot images)
   - `useSmartPollingConfig` returning `staleTime: 0` when realtime data is not fresh
   - React render cascade: parent re-render → non-memoized child re-render
   - `useSyncExternalStore` `subscribe` callback stability and `[observer, shouldSubscribe]` deps
   - `commitPassiveMountOnFiber` → `commitHookEffectListMount` → `subscribeToStore` cycle in React 18

Now I have the complete picture. Here's the root cause:

**Line 312 of `useSegmentOutputsForShot.ts`:**
```ts
const childrenQueryKey = [...segmentQueryKeys.children(selectedParentId!)] as string[];
```

This creates a **new array every render**. React Query's `observer.setOptions()` fires every render (via `useEffect`), and `shallowEqualObjects` sees `prevOptions.queryKey !== newOptions.queryKey` (reference equality fails). Combined with `staleTime: 0` from `useSmartPollingConfig` (when realtime data isn't fresh), this triggers:

1. Render → new queryKey → `observer.setOptions()` sees "changed" options
2. Query is stale (`staleTime: 0`) AND options "changed" → `executeFetch()`
3. Fetch → `isFetching: true` → observer notifies → re-render
4. Fetch completes → `setData` → `isFetching: false` → re-render
5. New render → new queryKey → goto 1

**= 15-18 renders/sec loop**

## Summary

**Root cause found and fixed**: An unstable query key array in `useSegmentOutputsForShot.ts` (line 312) was creating a new `string[]` reference on every render. React Query v5's `observer.setOptions()` uses `shallowEqualObjects` which compares `queryKey` by reference — so each render looked like "changed" options. Combined with `staleTime: 0` from `useSmartPollingConfig` (when realtime data isn't fresh), this triggered a continuous loop:

```
render → new queryKey → setOptions sees "changed" → staleTime: 0 → executeFetch 
→ isFetching: true → notify → re-render → fetch completes → setData → re-render → repeat
```

**Changes made:**

1. **`src/shared/hooks/segments/useSegmentOutputsForShot.ts`** — Memoized all three query keys (`parentQueryKey`, `childrenQueryKey`, `liveTimelineQueryKey`) with `useMemo` so they only change when their inputs (`shotId`, `projectId`, `selectedParentId`) change.

2. **`src/shared/hooks/useSmartPolling.ts`** — Memoized `useSmartPollingConfig` return value so the config object is stable when `refetchInterval` and `staleTime` don't change (defense-in-depth).

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-44 renders/sec) in the `travel-between-images` shot editor that makes the UI "glitchy." This has been an ongoing investigation across THREE sessions. The user explicitly said "it's okay to do a complete structural overhaul" and "go all the way" — meaning they want comprehensive structural fixes, not incremental patches. Previous sessions applied memoization fixes (statusRef in scheduleSave, ref-based callback deps, memoized returns) and the current session added query key memoization, but the loop persists. The user wants the actual ROOT CAUSE fixed with structural changes.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React Query v5.83.0 `useBaseQuery.ts` internals: `defaultedOptions` is a NEW object every render → `useEffect(() => observer.setOptions(defaultedOptions))` fires every render
   - `observer.setOptions()` uses `shallowEqualObjects` which compares `queryKey` by REFERENCE — unstable arrays cause "changed" detection
   - `shouldFetchOptionally` triggers refetch when query is stale (`staleTime: 0`) AND options "changed"
   - React Query structural sharing (`replaceEqualDeep`) breaks with `Map` objects → new reference every fetch
   - `useSyncExternalStore` subscription lifecycle — subscribe callback depends on `[observer, shouldSubscribe]`
   - React.memo for preventing parent re-render cascades
   - God hook anti-pattern: `useShotEditorController` (~900 lines) combines 12+ React Query observers in one render scope
   - Duplicate query observers: 4 observers for same query key (`generationQueryKeys.byShot(shotId)`)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/segments/useSegmentOutputsForShot.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Root cause of first fix attempt: unstable query keys created new arrays every render
     - Memoized all three query keys with `useMemo`:
     ```ts
     const parentQueryKey = useMemo(
       () => segmentQueryKeys.parents(shotId!, projectId ?? undefined),
       [shotId, projectId]
     );
     const childrenQueryKey = useMemo(
       () => [...segmentQueryKeys.children(selectedParentId!)] as string[],
       [selectedParentId]
     );
     const liveTimelineQueryKey = useMemo(
       () => segmentQueryKeys.liveTimeline(shotId!),
       [shotId]
     );
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useSmartPolling.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Added `useMemo` import and memoized `useSmartPollingConfig` return:
     ```ts
     return useMemo(() => ({
       refetchInterval,
       staleTime
     }), [refetchInterval, staleTime]);
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx`** — MODIFIED
     - Wrapped ShotSettingsEditor in `React.memo` to isolate from parent re-renders:
     ```ts
     const ShotSettingsEditor: React.FC<ShotEditorProps> = React.memo((props) => {
       const { hasSelectedShot, layoutProps } = useShotEditorController(props);
       if (!hasSelectedShot) {
         return (
           <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
             <p className="text-muted-foreground">Shot not found</p>
           </div>
     ```

## Task #7: Eliminate 3 duplicate shot image observers

The selector hooks (`useTimelineImages`, `useUnpositionedImages`, `useVideoOutputs`) each internally call `useAllShotGenerations(shotId)` — creating 3 additional React Query observers for the same query key as the `useShotImages` call. That's 4 observers total for one query. Each observer runs `observer.setOptions()` every render, amplifying the loop.

The fix: replace the 3 selector hook calls with inline `useMemo` filters on `allShotImages`/`fullShotImages` — the data is already available from the single `useShotImages` call.

## Task #8: Stabilize cache hook callbacks with refs

The `useProjectVideoCountsCache` callbacks have `projectCounts` (a `Map`) in their `useCallback` deps. Since Maps break React Query's structural sharing, `projectCounts` is a new reference on every refetch → callbacks are recreated → passed as props to `ShotSettingsEditor` → break React.memo.

Fix: use a ref for `projectCounts` so callbacks don't depend on it.

## Changes Summary

### Structural fixes to break render loop (4 layers addressed):

**1. `src/shared/hooks/useShotImages.ts`** — Removed `staleTime: 0` override
- Was making the query perpetually stale → every `observer.setOptions()` call triggered a refetch
- Replaced with `refetchOnMount: 'always'` to preserve fresh-data-on-open behavior

**2. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useShotEditorSetup.ts`** — Eliminated 3 duplicate React Query observers
- `useTimelineImages`, `useUnpositionedImages`, `useVideoOutputs` each internally called `useAllShotGenerations(shotId)` — 4 observers total for one query key
- Replaced with inline `useMemo` filters on the single `allShotImages` data source (4→1 observer)

**3. `src/shared/hooks/useProjectVideoCountsCache.ts`** — Stabilized callbacks with refs
- Map-based query data breaks React Query structural sharing → new reference on every refetch
- Callbacks had `projectCounts` in deps → recreated on every refetch → broke React.memo
- Now use `projectCountsRef` so callbacks only depend on `projectId`

**4. `src/shared/hooks/useProjectGenerationModesCache.ts`** — Same ref-based fix as above

**5. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx`** — `React.memo` on `ShotSettingsEditor`
- Prevents parent re-render cascades from reaching the 900-line controller

**6. `src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotEditorView.tsx`** — Removed redundant `useShotImages` observer
- The call at line 124 was discarded (result unused) — query already active in `useShotEditorSetup`
- Eliminated a source of parent-level re-renders on every query state change

**7. `src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — Skip idle→loading intermediate on entity change
- Entity changes now go directly to `loading` instead of idle→loading (saves one full re-render cascade per shot navigation)
- Updated both React Query and custom mode load effects to accept `loading` as a valid starting state

**8. `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.test.tsx`** — Updated test for React.memo

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-30 renders/sec) in the `travel-between-images` shot editor that causes visible UI flickering. This has been an ongoing investigation across THREE sessions. The user explicitly approved a "complete structural overhaul" approach. The MOST RECENT and CRITICAL finding from the user: **"still happening, it wasn't happening before the last git commit, can you see why?"** — meaning the render loop was INTRODUCED by the uncommitted changes (accumulated from previous refactoring sessions + this session's fixes). The last commit is `3db321c3`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React Query v5.83.0 internals: `observer.setOptions()` runs every render with new `defaultedOptions` object; `shallowEqualObjects` compares queryKey by REFERENCE
   - `staleTime: 0` makes queries perpetually stale → `setOptions` triggers refetch every render
   - React Query structural sharing (`replaceEqualDeep`) breaks with `Map` objects → new reference every fetch
   - React.memo prevents prop-change re-renders but NOT context-change re-renders
   - `VideoTravelSettingsContext` couples ALL consumers — any settings change triggers all focused hooks
   - `useShotEditorController` (~900 lines god hook) subscribes to context through 7+ focused hooks
   - `useAutoSaveSettings` state machine: idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving
   - Multiple React Query observers for the same query key amplify render cascades
   - Ref-based callback stabilization to prevent useCallback recreation from unstable deps
   - `useUserUIState` has 7+ instances of `privacyDefaults` across the component tree — they're VICTIMS of parent re-renders, not the cause

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useShotImages.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Removed `staleTime: 0` which made query perpetually stale causing fetch→render→fetch loop
     - Added `refetchOnMount: 'always'` to preserve fresh-data-on-open behavior
     ```ts
     // BEFORE:
     ...QUERY_PRESETS.realtimeBacked,
     staleTime: 0,
     // AFTER:
     ...QUERY_PRESETS.realtimeBacked,
     refetchOnMount: 'always',
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useShotEditorSetup.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Eliminated 3 duplicate React Query observers (4→1 observer)
     - Removed imports of `useTimelineImages`, `useUnpositionedImages`, `useVideoOutputs`
     - Replaced with inline `useMemo` filters on `allShotImages`:
     ```ts
     import { useShotImages } from '@/shared/hooks/useShotImages';
     // ... (removed useTimelineImages, useUnpositionedImages, useVideoOutputs)
     
     const timelineImages = useMemo(() => {
       return allShotImages
         .filter(g => {
           const location = g.imageUrl || g.location;
           const hasValidLocation = location && location !== '/placeholder.svg';
           return g.timeline_frame != null && g.timeline_frame >= 0 &&
                  !g.type?.includes('video') && hasValidLocation;
         })
         .sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0));
     }, [allShotImages]);
     
     const unpositionedImages = useMemo(() => {
       return allShotImages.filter(g => {
         const location = g.imageUrl || g.location;
         const hasValidLocation = location && location !== '/placeholder.svg';
         return g.timeline_frame == null && !g.type?.includes('video') && hasValidLocation;
       });
     }, [allShotImages]);
     
     const videoOutputs = useMemo(() => {
       return allShotImages.filter(g => g.type?.includes('video'));
     }, [allShotImages]);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useProjectVideoCountsCache.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Added `projectCountsRef` to stabilize callbacks (Maps break structural sharing)
     ```ts
     const projectCountsRef = useRef(projectCounts);
     projectCountsRef.current = projectCounts;
     // Changed callbacks from [projectId, projectCounts] to [projectId] deps
     // Using projectCountsRef.current?.get(shotId) instead of projectCounts?.get(shotId)
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useProjectGenerationModesCache.ts`** — MODIFIED

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx`** — MODIFIED (previous session)
     - Wrapped `ShotSettingsEditor` in `React.memo`

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.test.tsx`** — MODIFIED

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotEditorView.tsx`** — MODIFIED

     - Removed unused `isShotOperationInProgress` destructuring

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — MODIFIED
     - Entity change effect now goes directly to `loading` instead of `idle`:
     ```ts

     setStatus('idle');
     // AFTER:
     setStatus('loading');
     ```

     - Updated `useCustomModeLoad` to accept both 'idle' and 'loading':

After thoroughly analyzing every diff, I've found the most likely cause: an instability chain through `getLatestSettings` → `scheduleSave` → `debouncedSave`. Let me trace it:

1. Settings load from DB → `setSettings(data)` → `settings` reference changes
2. `getLatestSettings = useCallback(() => ({...settings}), [settings])` → recreated
3. `scheduleSave` depends on `getLatestSettings` → recreated  
4. `debouncedSave` return memoized on `scheduleSave` → changes
5. `debouncedSave` is in deps of ALL load effects → they re-run
6. Load effects bail out (deepEqual), but `debouncedSave` change propagates to `save` callback
7. `save` is in `useAutoSaveSettings` return memo → return changes
8. Context value changes → all consumers re-render → **loop restarts at step 1 if any consumer triggers settings changes**

Before our changes, `debouncedSave` was already unstable (un-memoized return), so this chain was invisible. But now with memoization, `debouncedSave` is "stably unstable" — it specifically triggers when settings change, creating a cascade.

The fix is to stabilize `getLatestSettings` by using the ref we already have:

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-30 renders/sec) in the `travel-between-images` shot editor that causes visible UI flickering when clicking into a shot. This has been investigated across THREE sessions. The user's MOST RECENT and CRITICAL statement: **"still happening, it wasn't happening before the last git commit, can you see why?"** — meaning the render loop was INTRODUCED by the uncommitted changes (accumulated from previous refactoring sessions + this session's fixes). The last commit is `3db321c3`. The user previously approved a "complete structural overhaul" approach and values thoroughness over speed.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React Query v5 observer mechanics: `observer.setOptions()` runs every render with `shallowEqualObjects` comparing queryKey by REFERENCE
   - `staleTime: 0` makes queries perpetually stale → combined with unstable options creates fetch→render→fetch loops
   - React Query structural sharing (`replaceEqualDeep`) breaks with `Map` objects → new reference every fetch
   - React.memo prevents prop-change re-renders but NOT context-change re-renders (useContext bypasses memo)
   - `VideoTravelSettingsContext` couples ALL consumers — any settings change triggers all focused hooks
   - `useShotEditorController` (~900 lines god hook) subscribes to context through 7+ focused hooks
   - `useAutoSaveSettings` state machine: idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving
   - Ref-based callback stabilization to prevent useCallback recreation from unstable deps
   - `useMemo` on hook return values to prevent object recreation causing effect/memo churn
   - `getLatestSettings` uses React setState identity trick (`setSettings(current => { resolve(current); return current; })`) for stable `[]` deps
   - `useDebouncedSettingsSave` return memoization with stable deps chain

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — MODIFIED (379 lines changed)
     - Core settings hook, drives the entire settings load/save lifecycle
     - Extracted `useCustomModeLoad` and `useReactQueryModeLoad` sub-hooks for readability
     - Entity change effect: `setStatus('idle')` → `setStatus('loading')` (skip one re-render cycle)
     - `saveImmediate` uses `settingsRef` instead of `settings` in deps
     - `save` uses `saveImmediateRef` instead of `saveImmediate` directly
     - Added `transitionReadyWithPendingSave` and `applyLoadedData` helper functions (NOT useCallback — plain functions)
     - Return memoized with `useMemo`
     - `getLatestSettings` already has stable `[]` deps (uses setState identity trick):
     ```ts
     const getLatestSettings = useCallback((): Promise<T> => {
       return new Promise<T>((resolve) => {
         setSettings(current => {
           resolve(current);
           return current; // Don't modify, just read
         });
       });
     }, []);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`** — MODIFIED (165 lines changed)
     - Manages debounced save scheduling, pending tracking, edit versioning, flush effects
     - `flushConfig` object flattened to individual props (`isCustomMode`, `scope`, `toolId`, `projectId`)
     - Added `statusRef` so `scheduleSave` reads status at call time without depending on it
     - Extracted `flushPendingSettings` as shared helper for entity-change cleanup and beforeunload
     - Return memoized with `useMemo`:
     ```ts
     return useMemo(() => ({
       scheduleSave, cancelPendingSave, clearPending, trackPendingUpdate,
       incrementEditVersion, hasPendingFor,
       pendingSettingsRef, pendingEntityIdRef, editVersionRef, saveTimeoutRef,
     }), [scheduleSave, cancelPendingSave, clearPending, trackPendingUpdate, incrementEditVersion, hasPendingFor]);
     ```

     ```ts
     return useMemo(() => ({
       settings: settings as T | undefined, isLoading,
       error: error as Error | null, update,
       isUpdating: updateMutation.isPending, hasShotSettings,
     }), [settings, isLoading, error, update, updateMutation.isPending, hasShotSettings]);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useShotImages.ts`** — MODIFIED (6 lines changed)
     - Changed `staleTime: 0` to use realtimeBacked preset's `staleTime: 30_000`

     ```ts
     staleTime: 0,

     ```

     ```ts
     const timelineImages = useMemo(() => {
       return allShotImages.filter(g => {
         const location = g.imageUrl || g.location;
         const hasValidLocation = location && location !== '/placeholder.svg';
         return g.timeline_frame != null && g.timeline_frame >= 0 &&
                !g.type?.includes('video') && hasValidLocation;
       }).sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0));
     }, [allShotImages]);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useProjectVideoCountsCache.ts`** — MODIFIED (40 lines changed)

     ```tsx
     function SettingsAutoDisable({ shotId, isCloudGenerationEnabled }: {
       shotId: string; isCloudGenerationEnabled: boolean;
     }) {
       const { settings, status, shotId: loadedShotId, updateField, updateFields } = useVideoTravelSettings();
       const { turboMode = false, advancedMode = false } = settings;
       useEffect(() => {
         if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
         if (!isCloudGenerationEnabled && turboMode) updateField('turboMode', false);
       }, [isCloudGenerationEnabled, turboMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateField]);
       useEffect(() => {
         if (status !== 'ready' || loadedShotId !== shotId) return;
         if (turboMode && advancedMode) updateFields({ advancedMode: false, motionMode: 'basic' });
       }, [turboMode, advancedMode, status, loadedShotId, shotId, updateFields]);
       return null;
     }
     ```

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants to fix a persistent render loop (15-30 renders/sec) in the `travel-between-images` shot editor that causes visible UI flickering when clicking into a shot. This has been investigated across FOUR sessions now. The user's MOST RECENT and CRITICAL statement (from prior session): **"still happening, it wasn't happening before the last git commit, can you see why?"** — meaning the render loop was INTRODUCED by the accumulated uncommitted changes (from previous refactoring sessions). The last commit is `3db321c3`. The user previously approved a "complete structural overhaul" approach and values thoroughness over speed.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React Query v5 observer mechanics: `observer.setOptions()` runs every render; structural sharing breaks with `Map` objects
   - React context coupling: `useContext` bypasses `React.memo` — when context value changes, ALL consumers re-render
   - `staleTime: 0` makes queries perpetually stale → combined with unstable options references creates fetch→render→fetch loops
   - Ref-based callback stabilization: using refs to avoid capturing state values in useCallback deps
   - `useMemo` on hook return values: prevents object recreation but must have stable deps
   - `useChangedDepsLogger`: dev-only diagnostic that logs which deps in a named map changed since previous render
   - Settings state machine: idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving (entity change resets to idle or loading)
   - `getLatestSettings` uses React setState identity trick for stable `[]` deps
   - `VideoTravelSettingsContext` couples ALL consumers — any settings/status change triggers all focused hooks
   - **Critical architectural insight**: In the COMMITTED code, `handlers` from `useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers` was a PLAIN OBJECT (new reference every render). This made `contextValue` useMemo in the provider ALWAYS recompute, so ALL context consumers re-rendered on EVERY parent render. In the CURRENT code, `handlers` is memoized → `contextValue` only changes on real dep changes. This is objectively better performance but somehow correlates with the flickering the user reports.

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** (625 lines, heavily modified)
     - Core settings hook driving the entire settings load/save lifecycle
     - Extracted `useCustomModeLoad` and `useReactQueryModeLoad` sub-hooks for readability
     - Key behavioral changes vs committed:
       - `saveImmediate` uses `settingsRef.current` instead of `settings` in deps (MORE STABLE)
       - `save` uses `saveImmediateRef.current` instead of `saveImmediate` (MORE STABLE)
       - Entity change: `setStatus('loading')` instead of `setStatus('idle')` (skip 1 render cycle)
       - `transitionReadyWithPendingSave` and `applyLoadedData` are plain functions (not useCallback), passed to sub-hooks but NOT in effect deps
     - **DIAGNOSTIC ADDED**: `useChangedDepsLogger` on return useMemo deps (force=true)
     ```ts
     useChangedDepsLogger(`AutoSaveSettings:${toolId}.return`, {
       settings, status, isDirty, error, isCustomMode, hasPersistedData, hasShotSettings,
       updateField, updateFields, save, saveImmediate, revert, reset, initializeFrom,
     }, true);
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`** (288 lines, heavily modified)
     - Manages debounced save scheduling, pending tracking, edit versioning, flush effects
     - Key changes: `statusRef` for stable `scheduleSave` (no `status` dep), return memoized with `useMemo`, `flushPendingSettings` extracted as shared helper
     - Committed version: `scheduleSave` depended on `status` → changed every status transition → `debouncedSave` always unstable
     - Current version: `scheduleSave` uses `statusRef.current` → stable → `debouncedSave` stable

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useToolSettings.ts`** (446 lines, modified)
     - Extracted `shouldRetrySettingsQuery`, `handleMutationSuccess`, `handleMutationError` as standalone functions
     - Return wrapped in `useMemo`
     - Uses `QUERY_PRESETS.static` with `staleTime: 10 * 60 * 1000`

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/useShotSettings.ts`** (281 lines, modified)
     - Added refs for callbacks (autoSaveSettingsRef, shotIdRef, projectIdRef)
     - Narrowed deps: `[autoSave]` → `[autoSave.updateField, autoSaveUpdateFields]`
     - **DIAGNOSTIC ADDED**: `useChangedDepsLogger` on return useMemo deps (force=true)
     ```ts
     useChangedDepsLogger('useShotSettings.return', {
       settings, status, entityId, isDirty, error, updateField,
       autoSaveUpdateFields, saveImmediate, revert,
       applyShotSettings, applyProjectDefaults, resetToDefaults,
     }, true);
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx`** (276 lines, modified)

     - **DIAGNOSTIC ADDED**: `useChangedDepsLogger` on contextValue deps (force=true)

     ```ts
       'settings': shotSettings.settings,
     ```

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - React state machine pattern: `idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving` for settings lifecycle
   - `statusRef` pattern: updating a ref both at render time AND immediately when `setStatus` is called, so effects in the same commit see the correct value
   - Cache-hit fast path: checking if React Query has cached data before resetting to defaults on entity change
   - `useCallback` + ref pattern for stable callbacks that read latest values
   - React 18 effect execution order: effects run in declaration order within the same commit
   - React Query structural sharing: preserves references for unchanged data parts, but fails with Maps
   - `useMemo` on hook return values to prevent cascading re-renders through context
   - `useChangedDepsLogger` with `force=true` for runtime diagnostic logging
   - Settings write queue with debounced saves and flush-on-unmount

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** (MODIFIED - this is the core file with the fixes)
     - This is the central settings hook that manages the entire load/save lifecycle
     - **Three changes made:**
     
     Change 1 - Added `statusRef` and `setStatus` wrapper (after line 322):
     ```ts
     const [status, setStatusRaw] = useState<AutoSaveStatus>('idle');
     // ...
     const statusRef = useRef(status);
     statusRef.current = status;
     const setStatus = useCallback((s: AutoSaveStatus) => {
       setStatusRaw(s);
       statusRef.current = s;
     }, []);
     ```
     
     Change 2 - Added `rqIsLoadingRef` and `dbSettingsRef` (after useToolSettings destructure):
     ```ts
     const rqIsLoadingRef = useRef(rqIsLoading);
     rqIsLoadingRef.current = rqIsLoading;
     const dbSettingsRef = useRef(dbSettings);
     dbSettingsRef.current = dbSettings;
     ```
     
     Change 3 - Cache-hit fast path in entity change effect:
     ```ts
     if (previousEntityId && previousEntityId !== entityId) {
       debouncedSave.clearPending();
       setHasPersistedData(false);
       if (!isCustomMode && !rqIsLoadingRef.current && dbSettingsRef.current) {
         // Fast path: cached data available, skip loading transition
         const loaded = { ...defaults, ...(dbSettingsRef.current as Record<string, unknown>) } as T;
         const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(loaded));
         setSettings(cloned);
         loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloned));
         setStatus('ready');
         setError(null);
       } else {
         // Slow path: show loading state
         setSettings(defaults);
         setStatus('loading');
         loadedSettingsRef.current = null;
       }
     }
     ```
     
     Change 4 - Updated both `useCustomModeLoad` and `useReactQueryModeLoad` sub-hooks:
     - Changed interface: `status: AutoSaveStatus` → `statusRef: React.MutableRefObject<AutoSaveStatus>`
     - Inside effects: replaced `status` with `statusRef.current` for all reads

     - `useReactQueryModeLoad` deps: `[isCustomMode, entityId, rqIsLoading, dbSettings, defaults, enabled, debouncedSave]` (no `status`)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Render-time state adjustment pattern**: Setting state during render (not in useEffect) so React discards the stale render before painting. See: https://react.dev/learn/you-might-not-need-an-effect#adjusting-some-state-when-a-prop-changes
   - **statusRef pattern**: Using a ref + wrapper `setStatus` that updates both React state and the ref, so effects in the same commit see the correct status value
   - **Ref-based callback stabilization**: Using `currentEntityIdRef.current` instead of `entityId` in useCallback deps to prevent callback recreation on entity changes
   - **React Query structural sharing**: Preserves references for unchanged data across refetches
   - **useStableSkeletonVisibility**: 120ms hide delay to prevent rapid skeleton flicker, but potentially causes the visual jolt
   - **PageFadeIn**: 300ms `animate-in fade-in` CSS animation on shot editor mount
   - **VideoTravelContent rendering model**: Old pattern uses a separate component with conditional rendering inside; current code still uses this pattern (merge kept local version)
   - **useDebouncedSettingsSave flush lifecycle**: Cleanup effect handles flushing pending saves for old entity when entityId changes — this is why render-time entity change code must NOT call `clearPending` (would null out refs before flush effect reads them)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** (MODIFIED - core file, all changes this session)
     - Central settings hook managing load/save lifecycle for all tool settings
     - Three changes made this session:
     
     **Change 1 — Entity-change effect → render-time state adjustment** (replaced lines ~565-608):
     ```ts
     // Handle entity changes during render (not in an effect).
     // This prevents the "stale frame" where the previous entity's settings flash briefly.
     // React will discard the current render and re-render with the new state before painting.
     //
     // NOTE: We intentionally do NOT call debouncedSave.clearPending() here.
     // The flush-on-entity-change cleanup in useDebouncedSettingsSave handles flushing
     // pending saves for the old entity and clearing refs — calling clearPending here
     // would null out the refs before the flush effect gets to read them.
     const previousEntityId = currentEntityIdRef.current;
     if (entityId !== previousEntityId) {
       currentEntityIdRef.current = entityId;
       if (!entityId) {
         setSettings(defaults);
         setStatus('idle');
         setHasPersistedData(false);
         loadedSettingsRef.current = null;
       } else if (previousEntityId) {
         setHasPersistedData(false);
         if (!isCustomMode && !rqIsLoadingRef.current && dbSettingsRef.current) {
           const loaded = { ...defaults, ...(dbSettingsRef.current as Record<string, unknown>) } as T;
           const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(loaded));
           setSettings(cloned);
           loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloned));
           setStatus('ready');
           setError(null);
         } else {
           setSettings(defaults);
           setStatus('loading');
           loadedSettingsRef.current = null;
         }
       }
       // else: entityId went from null to a value (first mount) — load effects handle initial load
     }
     ```
     
     **Change 2 — Stabilized `saveImmediate`** (uses ref instead of entityId dep):
     ```ts
     const saveImmediate = useCallback(async (settingsToSave?: T): Promise<void> => {
       const currentEntityId = currentEntityIdRef.current;
       if (!currentEntityId) { return; }
       // ... rest of implementation using currentEntityId ...
       if (isCustomMode) {
         await customSaveRef.current!(currentEntityId, toSave);
       }
       // ...
     }, [isCustomMode, updateSettings, scope, onSaveSuccess, onSaveError]);
     ```
     
     **Change 3 — Stabilized `updateField` and `updateFields`** (uses ref instead of entityId dep):
     ```ts
     const updateField = useCallback(<K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
       debouncedSave.incrementEditVersion();
       setSettings(prev => {
         const updated = { ...prev, [key]: value };
         debouncedSave.trackPendingUpdate(updated, currentEntityIdRef.current);
         debouncedSave.scheduleSave(currentEntityIdRef.current);
         return updated;
       });
     }, [debouncedSave]);
     ```

OK, I've traced the full rendering flow from click to editor. Here are the structural issues I found — any of these could be causing or contributing to the jolt:

## The Jolt: Root Cause Analysis

**The primary culprit is the `isModeReady` → skeleton gate.**

When you click a shot from the list, here's what happens frame-by-frame:

1. **Frame 1** — Shot list disappears, `ShotEditorView` mounts fresh
2. **Frame 1** — `useShotEditorState` initializes `isModeReady: false` (line 16, always `false`)
3. **Frame 1** — Inside `ShotImagesEditorSections` (line 398): `showSkeleton = !isModeReady || ...` → **skeleton renders**
4. **Frame 1 paints** — User sees skeleton inside the editor
5. **After paint** — `useModeReadiness` effects run. They check `settingsLoading` from context...
6. **But** `settingsLoading` is `true` because `useAutoSaveSettings` starts at `status: 'idle'`, and the provider says `isLoading: status === 'loading' || status === 'idle'`
7. **So `isModeReady` stays `false`** until settings finish their DB fetch
8. **Eventually** — React Query returns settings data → status becomes `'ready'` → `settingsLoading` becomes `false` → `useModeReadiness` sets `isModeReady: true` → skeleton replaced by actual content

**The skeleton shows for the entire duration of the settings DB fetch**, even though the timeline section only shows *images* (which are already available from navigation state via `usePrimeShotImagesCache`).

Before our stabilization work, the constant re-renders from duplicate hooks masked this transition — the skeleton → content swap happened amid so many other re-renders that it was invisible. Now each state transition is distinct.

## The Structural Gremlins

**Gremlin 1: `isModeReady` gates images on settings loading** — `useModeReadiness.ts:132` requires `criticalSettingsReady` (= `!settingsLoading`) even when images are available. The timeline shows images, not settings — it shouldn't need to wait.

**Gremlin 2: Three diagnostic `useChangedDepsLogger` calls with `force=true`** — These run expensive object comparison on every render at 3 points in the settings pipeline.

**Gremlin 3: `showSkeleton` is too aggressive** — `ShotImagesEditorSections.tsx:398` shows skeleton whenever `!isModeReady`, even if we have all the data needed to render.

All 3563 tests pass. Here's a summary of the three fixes:

## Changes Made

### Fix 1: `ShotImagesEditorSections.tsx` — Don't show skeleton when data exists
Changed the `showSkeleton` condition from `!isModeReady || ...` (always shows skeleton on first render) to `(!isModeReady && !data.memoizedShotGenerations.length) || ...`. Since `preloadedImages` (from `allShotImages` → `contextImages`) are available synchronously on first render, shots with images skip the skeleton entirely.

### Fix 2: `useModeReadiness.ts` — Remove settings-loading gate
Changed `if (hasImageData && criticalSettingsReady)` to `if (hasImageData)`. The timeline section shows images, not settings — it doesn't need to wait for the settings DB fetch to complete. Settings load in the background and the generation section uses its own loading check.

### Fix 3: Removed 3 diagnostic `useChangedDepsLogger` calls with `force=true`
These were running expensive object comparison on every render in:
- `useAutoSaveSettings.ts` — runs for EVERY tool's settings
- `useShotSettings.ts` — runs on the settings pipeline
- `VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx` — runs on every context recompute

These were added for debugging the render loop and are no longer needed.

The core insight: when clicking a shot from the list, `shotToEdit.images` is already available from the shots context. These images flow through `contextImages` → `allShotImages` → `preloadedImages` → `memoizedShotGenerations` all synchronously. But `isModeReady` always started at `false` and required a useEffect (after paint) + settings DB fetch to become `true`. This guaranteed a skeleton flash on every shot entry.

Found it. Lines 45-51 tell the whole story:

```
Line 44: 🎨 PAINTED transition: shot-list → EDITOR     ← editor appears
Line 45: currentShotId: undefined, shouldShowEditor: false  ← BOUNCES BACK
Line 46: → shot-list                                     ← shot list re-renders
Line 47: 🎨 PAINTED transition: EDITOR → shot-list       ← user sees shot list flash
Line 48: viaShotClick: true, shouldShowEditor: true       ← back to editor
Line 51: 🎨 PAINTED transition: shot-list → EDITOR       ← editor appears again
```

**The jolt is: EDITOR → shot-list → EDITOR in 3 paint frames.** The editor appears, then bounces back to the shot list for one frame, then goes back to the editor.

Root cause: **`useResetShotOnMount` is firing during a race condition.** When you click a shot:

1. Click handler calls `setCurrentShotId(shotId)` — immediate context update
2. Click handler calls `navigate(url, { state: { fromShotClick: true } })` — async router update
3. React renders with `currentShotId = shotId` BUT `viaShotClick = false` (old location state)
4. Effect runs: `!viaShotClick && !hasHashShotId && currentShotId` → **clears currentShotId!**
5. Router catches up: `viaShotClick = true` → editor recovers from hash

The effect name says "OnMount" but it re-fires on every dependency change including `currentShotId`:

> TOOL

tool_use Read
```json
reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/videoTravelPageModel.tsx
```

> TOOL

tool_use Read
```json
reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/videoTravelPageModel.tsx
```

> AGENT

There it is. `useResetShotOnMount` re-fires on every `currentShotId` change (line 189), not just on mount. When the click handler sets `currentShotId` before the router sets `viaShotClick`, the effect sees `!viaShotClick && currentShotId` and clears it.

The fix: make it truly run only on mount by capturing initial values:

> TOOL

```json

```
