Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user has been working across 5+ sessions to fix a visual "jolt" / flickering when clicking into a shot from the shot list in the `travel-between-images` tool. The jolt manifests as a brief flash where the editor appears, bounces back to the shot list for one paint frame, then returns to the editor. The user has confirmed:
   - It ONLY happens when clicking from the shot list (not chevron navigation between shots, not direct URL visit)
   - It wasn't happening before the uncommitted changes from previous Claude sessions
   - The merge commit `3db321c3` is identical to its local parent (empty diff) — the jolt comes from ~1800 lines of uncommitted stabilization changes

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Render-time state adjustment pattern**: Setting state during render so React discards stale render before painting (from React docs: "you-might-not-need-an-effect")
   - **statusRef pattern**: Using a ref + wrapper `setStatus` for effects to read correct status in same commit
   - **Ref-based callback stabilization**: Using `currentEntityIdRef.current` instead of `entityId` in useCallback deps
   - **Race condition between React context and React Router**: `setCurrentShotId` (context state) updates immediately, but `navigate()` (router) is async — causes intermediate render with stale location state
   - **`isModeReady` gating**: Editor uses `isModeReady` (starts `false`) to show skeleton, requiring useEffect to set `true`
   - **`usePrimeShotImagesCache`**: Primes React Query cache via `useEffect` (NOT synchronous), but `allShotImages` falls back to `contextImages` synchronously
   - **`useStableSkeletonVisibility`**: 120ms hide delay for page-level skeleton
   - **Paint frame tracking**: Using `requestAnimationFrame` after branch change to log actual browser paints

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/videoTravelPageModel.tsx`** — CRITICAL: Contains `VideoTravelContent` (conditional rendering router) and `useResetShotOnMount` (the identified race condition source)
     - Added diagnostic logging with branch tracking and paint frame detection
     - Fixed `useResetShotOnMount` to use initial ref values and run only on mount (BUT FIX DIDN'T WORK - bounce still happening)
     ```tsx
     export function useResetShotOnMount(
       hash: string,
       viaShotClick: boolean,
       currentShotId: string | null,
       setCurrentShotId: (shotId: string | null) => void,
     ) {
       const initialRef = useRef({ hash, viaShotClick, currentShotId });
       useEffect(() => {
         const { hash: h, viaShotClick: v, currentShotId: c } = initialRef.current;
         const hasHashShotId = !!h?.replace('#', '');
         if (!v && !hasHashShotId && c) {
           setCurrentShotId(null);
         }
       }, [setCurrentShotId]);
     }
     ```
     - Diagnostic logs still in place:
     ```tsx
     const branch = !selectedProjectId ? 'no-project'
       : shotsError ? 'error'
       : showStableSkeleton ? 'skeleton'
       : shouldShowEditor ? (shotToEdit ? 'EDITOR' : ...) : 'shot-list';
     console.log(`[VideoTravelContent] → ${branch}`, {...});
     // Paint tracking:
     if (branch !== prevBranchRef.current) {
       requestAnimationFrame(() => {
         console.log(`[VideoTravelContent] 🎨 PAINTED transition: ${from} → ${to}`);
       });
     }
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/VideoTravelToolPage.tsx`** — Main page component
     - Added diagnostic log tracking key routing state
     ```tsx
     console.log('[VideoTravelToolPage] render', {
       viaShotClick, currentShotId, shouldShowEditor, hasShotToEdit, isLoading, showStableSkeleton, shotsLoading, shotsCount
     });
     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotEditorView.tsx`** — Shot editor wrapper
     - Removed duplicate `useShotSettings` and `useShotImages` calls (previous sessions)
     - Added `SettingsAutoDisable` renderless component inside provider
     - Removed `PageFadeIn` wrapper (this session — didn't fix jolt)
     - Added diagnostic mount/render logging
     ```tsx

     console.log('[ShotEditorView] render', {
       shotId: shotToEdit.id.slice(0, 8),
       age: Math.round(performance.now() - mountTimeRef.current) + 'ms',

     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotImagesEditor/ShotImagesEditorSections.tsx`** — Contains skeleton gating logic
     - Changed `showSkeleton` condition to not require `isModeReady` when data is present:
     ```tsx

     // After:

     ```

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useModeReadiness.ts`** — Controls `isModeReady` flag

     ```tsx
       return;

     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** — Central settings hook (674 lines)
     - Removed diagnostic `useChangedDepsLogger` call with `force=true`
     - Removed `useChangedDepsLogger` from import
     - Contains render-time entity change code, stabilized callbacks (from previous sessions in this context)

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/useShotSettings.ts`** — Shot-specific settings

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx`** — Settings context provider
     - Removed diagnostic `useChangedDepsLogger` call with `force=true`

     - `usePrimeShotImagesCache` uses `React.useEffect` (NOT synchronous) — primes cache AFTER paint

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/state/useShotEditorState.ts`** — `isModeReady` initializes to `false`

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useShotEditorSetup.ts`** — Image derivation from `allShotImages`

Now I can see the full picture. Let me trace the exact sequence for the second click (shot `4be72ce7`, lines 77-93):

```
Line 77: viaShotClick: false, currentShotId: '4be72ce7'  → EDITOR (setCurrentShotId fired, but navigate hasn't committed yet!)
Line 86: 🎨 PAINTED shot-list → EDITOR                    (first paint)
Line 87: viaShotClick: false, currentShotId: undefined     → shot-list  (useUrlSync CLEARED it!)
Line 89: 🎨 PAINTED EDITOR → shot-list                    (JOLT)
Line 90: viaShotClick: true, currentShotId: undefined      → EDITOR  (navigate finally committed)
Line 93: 🎨 PAINTED shot-list → EDITOR                    (recovery)
```

**Root cause found.** In `useShotNavigation.ts`, `navigateToShot` calls `setCurrentShotId(shot.id)` THEN `navigate(url)`. These are NOT batched — `setCurrentShotId` produces an immediate render where `currentShotId` is set but `location.hash` is still empty and `viaShotClick` is still false. Then `useUrlSync` sees "no hash, has selectedShot, not viaShotClick" and clears `currentShotId` to null:

```tsx
// useUrlSync.ts line 50-52
if (!currentHashShotId && selectedShot && !viaShotClick) {
  setCurrentShotId(null);  // ← THIS IS THE CULPRIT
  return;
}
```

**The fix**: Remove `setCurrentShotId(shot.id)` from `navigateToShot()`. The `navigate()` call sets the hash and `fromShotClick` state — `useSelectedShotResolution` already resolves the shot from `hashShotId` and `shotFromState`, so `currentShotId` is redundant during navigation. The sync hooks (`useUrlSync`, `useSyncCurrentShotId`) will set `currentShotId` from the hash after navigate commits.

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user has been working across 5+ sessions to fix visual issues in the `travel-between-images` tool's shot editor. This session addressed three issues sequentially:
   
   **Issue 1 (FIXED)**: A visual "jolt" (EDITOR → shot-list → EDITOR bounce) when clicking into a shot from the shot list. Root cause: `setCurrentShotId()` and `navigate()` in `navigateToShot()` are not batched by React, causing an intermediate render where `useUrlSync` clears the shot ID.
   
   **Issue 2 (FIXED)**: After fixing the jolt, clicking into a shot caused the shot list to visibly scroll to the top before the editor appeared. Root cause: `preScrollToTopIfNeeded` fired synchronously before `navigate()` committed.
   
   **Issue 3 (IN PROGRESS)**: Persistent render loops at 15-20 renders/sec affecting `AutoSaveSettings:join-segments`, `DebouncedSettingsSave:join-segments`, `UserUIState:privacyDefaults`, and `UserUIState:generationMethods`. User explicitly said "get to the very bottom of it" — wants thorough root cause analysis, not surface-level fixes.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **React state batching**: `setCurrentShotId()` (context) and `navigate()` (router) are NOT batched by React — they produce separate renders, causing race conditions
   - **`useLayoutEffect` vs `requestAnimationFrame`**: `useLayoutEffect` fires synchronously after DOM commit before paint (correct for scroll-to-top); `requestAnimationFrame` fires before next paint but AFTER event handler returns (too early for navigate transitions)
   - **Hash-driven shot resolution**: `useSelectedShotResolution` resolves `shotToEdit` from `hashShotId` + `shotFromState` without needing `currentShotId` — the hash is the source of truth
   - **React Query re-subscription cycle**: Stack traces in logs show `commitHookEffectListMount → subscribeToStore → executeFetch_fn → setData → dispatch_fn → flushSyncCallbacks` causing cascading renders when query observers re-subscribe
   - **Settings architecture**: `useToolSettings` (React Query) → `useAutoSaveSettings` (state + persistence) → `useJoinSegmentsSettings` / `useShotSettings` (tool-specific) → `VideoTravelSettingsProvider` (context)

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Removed `preScrollToTopIfNeeded` function — caused visible scroll on shot list before editor appeared
     - Current state of `navigateToShot`:
     ```tsx
     const navigateToShot = (shot: Shot, options: ShotNavigationOptions = {}) => {
       const opts = { ...defaultOptions, ...options };
       // NOTE: We intentionally do NOT call setCurrentShotId() here.
       // navigate() and setCurrentShotId() are not batched by React — the context
       // update renders before the router update, creating an intermediate frame
       // where currentShotId is set but location.hash is empty. useUrlSync then
       // clears currentShotId, causing a visible EDITOR → shot-list → EDITOR jolt.
       // Instead, we let the hash drive everything: useSelectedShotResolution
       // resolves shotToEdit from hashShotId + shotFromState, and useUrlSync/
       // useSyncCurrentShotId set currentShotId from the hash after navigation.
       const targetUrl = travelShotUrl(shot.id);
       navigate(targetUrl, {
         state: {
           fromShotClick: true,
           shotData: shot,
           isNewlyCreated: opts.isNewlyCreated
         },
         replace: opts.replace,
       });
       performScroll(opts);
       closePanes(opts);
     };
     ```

   - **`src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useShotNavigation.test.ts`** — Updated tests
     - Changed 3 tests from `expect(mockSetCurrentShotId).toHaveBeenCalledWith(...)` to `expect(mockSetCurrentShotId).not.toHaveBeenCalled()`

   - **`src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/videoTravelPageModel.tsx`** — Cleanup + scroll fix
     - Removed diagnostic console.logs (branch tracking, paint frame tracking)
     - Removed unused `useMemo` import
     - Renamed `useScrollToTopOnHashExit` → `useScrollToTopOnHashChange` with expanded logic:
     ```tsx
     export function useScrollToTopOnHashChange(hash: string) {
       const prevHashRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
       useLayoutEffect(() => {
         const hasHash = hash && hash.length > 1;
         const hadHash = prevHashRef.current !== null && prevHashRef.current.length > 1;
         const isFirstRender = prevHashRef.current === null;
         // Scroll to top when toggling between shot list (no hash) and editor (hash).
         // useLayoutEffect fires synchronously after DOM commit, before paint.
         // Does NOT fire for hash→hash changes (chevron navigation handles its own scroll).
         if ((isFirstRender && !hasHash) || (!isFirstRender && hasHash !== hadHash)) {
           window.scrollTo(0, 0);
           window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('app:scrollToTop', { detail: { behavior: 'auto' } }));
         }
         prevHashRef.current = hash;
       }, [hash]);
     }
     ```
     - Cleaned up `useResetShotOnMount` comment (removed stale race condition explanation)

**Root cause**: A React Query `setData` event triggers a render cascade through the component tree. Because `ShotSettingsEditor` has React.memo but receives 6 unstable prop references that change on every `ShotEditorView` render, the memo check ALWAYS fails, causing the massive `useShotEditorController` (with 20+ React Query hooks) to re-run. 5. `onUpdateShotName` — depends on `updateShotNameMutation` (new object from `useMutation`)

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user has been working across 6+ sessions to fix visual and performance issues in the `travel-between-images` tool's shot editor. This session focused on:
   
   **Issue 1 (FIXED in previous sessions)**: Visual "jolt" (EDITOR → shot-list → EDITOR bounce) when clicking a shot. Fixed by removing `setCurrentShotId()` from `navigateToShot()` in `useShotNavigation.ts`.
   
   **Issue 2 (FIXED in previous sessions)**: Scroll jump when clicking into a shot. Fixed by expanding `useScrollToTopOnHashChange` to use `useLayoutEffect` and passing `{ scrollToTop: false }` from shot list clicks.
   
   **Issue 3 (ACTIVELY BEING FIXED)**: Persistent render loops at 15-20 renders/sec. The user explicitly said "get to the very bottom of it" — wanting thorough root cause analysis and complete fixes. The affected hooks are:
   - `AutoSaveSettings:join-segments` — 15-19 renders/sec
   - `DebouncedSettingsSave:join-segments` — same
   - `UserUIState:privacyDefaults` — 15-20 renders/sec  
   - `UserUIState:generationMethods` — 15-20 renders/sec

   **Unimplemented plan exists**: A plan file at `/user_c042661f/.claude/plans/fuzzy-waddling-sparrow.md` describes fixing a double-load of settings (two `useShotSettings` instances in `ShotEditorView`). This is a separate issue from the render loop.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **React.memo prop instability**: When memoized components receive new object/function references as props, the memo check fails and the component re-renders
   - **React Query `useSyncExternalStore` re-subscription cycle**: `commitHookEffectListMount → subscribeToStore → subscribe → onSubscribe → executeFetch_fn → fetch → resolve → setData → dispatch_fn → batch → flush → flushSyncCallbacks` creates a self-sustaining render loop
   - **`useMutation` reference instability**: React Query's `useMutation` returns a new result object on every render; using it in `useCallback` deps causes callback recreation
   - **`useQuery` refetch instability**: The `refetch` function from `useQuery` is created via `this.refetch.bind(this)` in `createResult`, producing a new reference each render
   - **Ref pattern for stable callbacks**: Using `useRef` to store mutable values and `useCallback([])` with empty deps creates permanently stable function references
   - **Map objects break structural sharing**: React Query's `structuralSharing` can't compare Map objects, so every refetch produces a new data reference
   - **Settings architecture**: `useToolSettings` (React Query) → `useAutoSaveSettings` (state + persistence) → tool-specific hooks → providers
   - **QUERY_PRESETS.static**: `staleTime: 5min`, `gcTime: 15min`, `refetchOnWindowFocus: false`. Does NOT set `refetchOnMount: false`.
   - React 18.3.1 with @tanstack/react-query ^5.56.2

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/hooks/useShotNavigation.ts`** — REWRITTEN to fix render loop

     - These were used as `useCallback` dependencies in `ShotEditorView`, cascading instability
     - Fix: Refs for `navigate`, `setCurrentShotId`, `isMobile`; all functions wrapped in `useCallback([])`; return memoized; helper functions extracted to module scope

All 281 test files, 1190 tests pass — zero failures.

Here's a summary of the three render loop fixes applied:

### Render loop root cause
`ShotSettingsEditor` is wrapped in `React.memo`, but 6 props from `ShotEditorView` were unstable (new references every render). This caused memo to always fail, triggering the massive `useShotEditorController` (20+ React Query hooks) to re-run, creating a self-sustaining re-subscription → re-fetch → setData → re-render cycle at 15-20/sec.

### Fixes applied

1. **`useShotNavigation.ts`** (applied last session) — Stabilized all 4 navigation callbacks by storing `navigate`, `setCurrentShotId`, and `isMobile` in refs, using `useCallback([])` with empty deps, and wrapping the return in `useMemo`. Fixes props: `onPreviousShot`, `onNextShot`, `onPreviousShotNoScroll`, `onNextShotNoScroll`.

2. **`ShotEditorView.tsx`** (applied now) — Added `updateShotNameMutateRef` for `updateShotNameMutation.mutate`. The `useMutation` result is a new object every render; using a ref makes `handleUpdateShotName` stable with only `[shotToEdit.id, selectedProjectId]` as deps.

3. **`useProjectVideoCountsCache.ts`** (applied now) — Changed `invalidateOnVideoChanges` to call `refetchRef.current()` instead of `refetch()`, removing `refetch` from the deps array. `useQuery`'s `refetch` is re-bound in `createResult` every render; the ref was already added last session but the callback wasn't updated to use it.

With all 6 unstable props now stable, `ShotSettingsEditor`'s `React.memo` will properly skip re-renders when props haven't semantically changed, breaking the render loop.
